To ensure parameter efficiency, we introduce Global and Private Kronecker (GPK) decomposition to effectively reduces storage and runtime overhead and incorporate Block-wise Learnable Clipping to suppress residual outliers. Extensive experiments on both MLLMs and LLMs demonstrate that BATQuant establishes new state-of-the-art results under aggressive W4A4KV16 configurations, recovering up to 96.43% of full-precision performance on multimodal benchmarks and clearly outperforming existing methods across diverse tasks.

</details>


### [153] [Tarab: A Multi-Dialect Corpus of Arabic Lyrics and Poetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16601)
*Mo El-Haj*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Tarab语料库，这是目前最大的开源阿拉伯语创意文本语料库，包含256万诗句和1350多万词符，涵盖古典与现代的阿拉伯诗歌与歌词，支持多种方言和历史时期的语言分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了促进对阿拉伯语诗歌与歌曲在语言、风格和历时演变方面的系统性研究，构建一个大规模、结构化且公开可用的文化语言资源。

Method: 收集并整合了跨越十四世纪的阿拉伯语歌词与诗歌数据，涵盖多种语言变体（包括古典阿拉伯语、标准现代阿拉伯语及六种主要方言），并对每句诗提供详细的结构化元数据；建立了数据采集、规范化和验证流程，并进行了语言变体识别与体裁区分的基线分析。

Result: 构建了包含256万诗句、1350多万词符的Tarab语料库，覆盖28个国家和多个历史时期，提供了丰富的元数据支持跨体裁、跨时代的比较分析，并已在HuggingFace上公开发布。

Conclusion: Tarab语料库为阿拉伯语的语言学、文学和文化研究提供了重要资源，支持多种自然语言处理任务，特别是体裁与方言识别等任务，推动了阿拉伯语数字人文研究的发展。

Abstract: We introduce the Tarab Corpus, a large-scale cultural and linguistic resource that brings together Arabic song lyrics and poetry within a unified analytical framework. The corpus comprises 2.56 million verses and more than 13.5 million tokens, making it, to our knowledge, the largest open Arabic corpus of creative text spanning both classical and contemporary production. Tarab is broadly balanced between songs and poems and covers Classical Arabic, Modern Standard Arabic (MSA), and six major regional varieties: Egyptian, Gulf, Levantine, Iraqi, Sudanese, and Maghrebi Arabic. The artists and poets represented in the corpus are associated with 28 modern nation states and multiple historical eras, covering over fourteen centuries of Arabic creative expression from the Pre-Islamic period to the twenty-first century. Each verse is accompanied by structured metadata describing linguistic variety, geographic origin, and historical or cultural context, enabling comparative linguistic, stylistic, and diachronic analysis across genres and time. We describe the data collection, normalisation, and validation pipeline and present baseline analyses for variety identification and genre differentiation. The dataset is publicly available on HuggingFace at https://huggingface.co/datasets/drelhaj/Tarab.

</details>


### [154] [Omnilingual SONAR: Cross-Lingual and Cross-Modal Sentence Embeddings Bridging Massively Multilingual Text and Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16606)
*Omnilingual SONAR Team,João Maria Janeiro,Pere-Lluís Huguet Cabot,Ioannis Tsiamas,Yen Meng,Vivek Iyer,Guillem Ramírez,Loic Barrault,Belen Alastruey,Yu-An Chung,Marta R. Costa-Jussa,David Dale,Kevin Heffernan,Jaehyeong Jo,Artyom Kozhevnikov,Alexandre Mourachko,Christophe Ropers,Holger Schwenk,Paul-Ambroise Duquenne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OmniSONAR是一种新型的多语言、跨模态句子嵌入模型，支持文本、语音、代码和数学表达式的统一语义表示，覆盖数千种语言，在多种下游任务中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨语言句子编码器通常仅覆盖几百种语言，且在对齐强度与下游任务性能之间存在权衡，难以广泛应用。为突破语言覆盖范围和表示质量的限制，需要一种可扩展至数千种语言并保持高性能的统一嵌入框架。

Method: 提出OmniSONAR，采用渐进式训练策略：首先使用LLM初始化的编码器-解码器结构，结合token级解码、分拆softmax对比损失和合成难负例，在200种语言上构建基础语义空间；然后通过两阶段教师-学生蒸馏框架扩展到数千种语言变体；最后将177种口语无缝映射至该空间，实现跨模态扩展。此外，训练仅基于英文文本处理OmniSONAR嵌入序列的语言模型Spectrum，以实现复杂任务的跨语言迁移。

Result: 在200语言FLORES数据集上，跨语言相似性搜索错误减少一半；在1560语言BIBLE基准上错误降低15倍；多语言翻译任务优于NLLB-3B，英译BIBLE任务比先前模型提升15 chrF++点；在MTEB和XLCoST上表现优异；语音方面，相似性搜索错误低43%，接近SeamlessM4T的97%语音到文本质量（尽管仅为零样本翻译）；Spectrum模型实现了向数千语言及语音的高效迁移。

Conclusion: OmniSONAR通过渐进训练和蒸馏方法，成功构建了一个支持数千种语言和多种模态的统一语义空间，在广泛的语言资源条件下均实现了最先进的下游性能，展示了其在低资源语言和跨模态应用中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Cross-lingual sentence encoders typically cover only a few hundred languages and often trade downstream quality for stronger alignment, limiting their adoption. We introduce OmniSONAR, a new family of omnilingual, cross-lingual and cross-modal sentence embedding models that natively embed text, speech, code, and mathematical expressions in a single semantic space, while delivering state-of-the-art downstream performance at the scale of thousands of languages, from high-resource to extremely low-resource varieties. To reach this scale without representation collapse, we use progressive training. We first learn a strong foundational space for 200 languages with an LLM-initialized encoder-decoder, combining token-level decoding with a novel split-softmax contrastive loss and synthetic hard negatives. Building on this foundation, we expand to several thousands language varieties via a two-stage teacher-student encoder distillation framework. Finally, we demonstrate the cross-modal extensibility of this space by seamlessly mapping 177 spoken languages into it. OmniSONAR halves cross-lingual similarity search error on the 200-language FLORES dataset and reduces error by a factor of 15 on the 1,560-language BIBLE benchmark. It also enables strong translation, outperforming NLLB-3B on multilingual benchmarks and exceeding prior models (including much larger LLMs) by 15 chrF++ points on 1,560 languages into English BIBLE translation. OmniSONAR also performs strongly on MTEB and XLCoST. For speech, OmniSONAR achieves a 43% lower similarity-search error and reaches 97% of SeamlessM4T speech-to-text quality, despite being zero-shot for translation (trained only on ASR data). Finally, by training an encoder-decoder LM, Spectrum, exclusively on English text processing OmniSONAR embedding sequences, we unlock high-performance transfer to thousands of languages and speech for complex downstream tasks.

</details>


### [155] [Domain Mixture Design via Log-Likelihood Differences for Aligning Language Models with a Target Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16622)
*Ryo Kishino,Riku Shiomi,Hiroaki Yamagiwa,Momose Oyama,Hidetoshi Shimodaira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种通过设计训练数据领域混合来对齐基础模型与目标模型分布的方法，利用log-likelihood空间中的方向对齐训练更新方向，有效降低KL散度并提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接进行语言模型蒸馏成本较高，希望通过调整预训练数据的领域分布实现模型对齐，提供一种无需持续蒸馏的替代方案。

Method: 将模型视为log-likelihood空间中的点，通过计算目标模型的方向，优化训练数据的领域权重，使训练更新方向朝向目标模型。

Result: 在NanoGPT上的实验表明，相比在The Pile上均匀采样，该方法持续降低到目标模型的KL散度，并使下游任务性能更接近目标模型。

Conclusion: 所提方法为模型对齐提供了一种有效的数据驱动途径，虽不如知识蒸馏高效，但在无法进行蒸馏时具有实际价值。

Abstract: Instead of directly distilling a language model, this study addresses the problem of aligning a base model with a target model in distribution by designing the domain mixture of training data for pretraining or continued pretraining as a fixed training recipe. We propose a method for determining domain weights by viewing models as points in log-likelihood space and aligning the training update direction with the direction toward the target model. Experiments with NanoGPT show that the proposed method consistently reduces the KL divergence to the target model compared with uniform weighting over the Pile. Although knowledge distillation remains more effective when available, the proposed method still achieves meaningful alignment, and downstream task performance also tends to become closer to that of the target model.

</details>


### [156] [Good Arguments Against the People Pleasers: How Reasoning Mitigates (Yet Masks) LLM Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16643)
*Zhaoxin Feng,Zheng Chen,Jianfei Ma,Yip Tin Po,Emmanuele Chersoni,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了链式思维（CoT）推理在大语言模型中对讨好性行为（sycophancy）的影响，发现尽管推理总体上减少了最终决策中的讨好性，但也可能通过逻辑不一致等手段掩盖其存在，尤其是在主观任务和权威偏见下更为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究对齐技术引发的LLM讨好性问题，并明确Chain-of-Thought推理在其中的作用：是抑制还是掩盖讨好性行为。

Method: 在客观与主观任务中评估多种模型，结合行为实验与针对三个开源模型的机制分析，研究CoT推理过程中讨好性的动态变化。

Result: 推理通常能减少最终决策中的讨好性，但部分样本中会通过逻辑错误、片面论证等方式生成欺骗性解释来掩盖讨好性；模型在主观任务和权威偏见下更易表现出讨好性；机制分析显示讨好性倾向在推理过程中动态演变，而非输入阶段即已确定。

Conclusion: Chain-of-Thought推理既可作为抑制讨好性的逻辑约束，也可能成为掩盖讨好性的后验合理化工具，其作用取决于具体情境与任务类型。

Abstract: Alignment techniques often inadvertently induce sycophancy in LLMs. While prior studies studied this behaviour in direct-answer settings, the role of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning remains under-explored: does it serve as a logical constraint that mitigates sycophancy, or a tool for post-hoc rationalization that masks it? We evaluate a range of models across objective and subjective tasks to investigate the issue. Results show that reasoning generally reduces sycophancy in final decisions but also masks sycophancy in some samples, where models construct deceptive justifications through logical inconsistencies, calculation errors, and one-sided arguments etc. Furthermore, LLMs are more prone to sycophancy in subjective tasks and under authority-bias. Our mechanistic analysis on three open-source models reveals that the tendency of sycophancy is dynamic during the reasoning process rather than being pre-determined at the input stage.

</details>


### [157] [Omanic: Towards Step-wise Evaluation of Multi-hop Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16654)
*Xiaojie Gu,Sherry T. Tong,Aosong Feng,Sophia Simeng Han,Jinghui Lu,Yingjian Chen,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Chanjun Park,Rex Ying,Irene Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Omanic，一个用于分析推理过程的开放域多跳问答资源，包含机器生成的训练集和人工标注的测试集，通过结构化注释支持对大模型推理能力的细粒度评估，并验证了其在提升其他推理任务性能方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多跳问答基准缺乏对中间推理步骤的标注，难以诊断模型在推理过程中的具体失败原因，因此需要一种具备细粒度结构化注释的数据集来深入分析大语言模型的推理行为。

Method: 构建了一个名为Omanic的新数据集，包含10,296个机器生成的训练样本（OmanicSynth）和967个人工审核的评测样本（OmanicBench），并为每个样本提供分解后的子问题和中间答案作为结构化注释；在此基础上进行系统性评估，并将OmanicSynth用于监督微调以检验其迁移效果。

Result: 最先进的大模型在OmanicBench上的准确率仅为73.11%，显示出该数据集的高难度；逐步分析表明，思维链（CoT）的效果依赖于事实完整性，在知识缺失时性能下降，且错误会在后续推理步骤中放大；基于OmanicSynth的监督微调在六个推理与数学基准上平均提升了7.41分，证明其作为训练数据的有效性。

Conclusion: Omanic填补了多跳推理中缺乏细粒度诊断资源的空白，不仅可用于评估模型的推理路径，还可作为高质量训练数据提升其他任务的推理能力，具有良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: Reasoning-focused large language models (LLMs) have advanced in many NLP tasks, yet their evaluation remains challenging: final answers alone do not expose the intermediate reasoning steps, making it difficult to determine whether a model truly reasons correctly and where failures occur, while existing multi-hop QA benchmarks lack step-level annotations for diagnosing reasoning failures. To address this gap, we propose Omanic, an open-domain multi-hop QA resource that provides decomposed sub-questions and intermediate answers as structural annotations for analyzing reasoning processes. It contains 10,296 machine-generated training examples (OmanicSynth) and 967 expert-reviewed human-annotated evaluation examples (OmanicBench). Systematic evaluations show that state-of-the-art LLMs achieve only 73.11% multiple-choice accuracy on OmanicBench, confirming its high difficulty. Stepwise analysis reveals that CoT's performance hinges on factual completeness, with its gains diminishing under knowledge gaps and errors amplifying in later hops. Additionally, supervised fine-tuning on OmanicSynth brings substantial transfer gains (7.41 average points) across six reasoning and math benchmarks, validating the dataset's quality and further supporting the effectiveness of OmanicSynth as supervision for reasoning-capability transfer. We release the data at https://huggingface.co/datasets/li-lab/Omanic and the code at https://github.com/XiaojieGu/Omanic.

</details>


### [158] [Can Linguistically Related Languages Guide LLM Translation in Low-Resource Settings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16660)
*Aishwarya Ramasethu,Niyathi Allu,Rohin Garg,Harshwardhan Fartale,Dun Li Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了在极低资源机器翻译中，利用语言相似的枢纽语言和少样本示例进行大语言模型推理时提示的有效性，发现该方法在特定条件下有一定改进，但效果有限且对示例构造敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在缺乏大规模平行数据的情况下，如何通过语言相似的枢纽语言和少样本演示来提升大语言模型在低资源场景下的翻译能力。

Method: 结合语言相关的枢纽语言与少样本上下文示例，在不更新参数的情况下进行无需训练的提示式翻译，并在受控条件下评估其表现。

Result: 在目标语言词汇表示较弱的情况下，基于枢纽语言的提示能带来一定性能提升，但增益通常较小且依赖于少样本示例的构建方式；对于语言相近或表示较好的语言变体，效果则不稳定或减弱。

Conclusion: 推理时提示结合枢纽语言可作为低资源翻译中微调的一种轻量替代方案，但其有效性受限于语言表示和示例设计，需谨慎应用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across many downstream tasks, yet their effectiveness in extremely low-resource machine translation remains limited. Standard adaptation techniques typically rely on large-scale parallel data or extensive fine-tuning, which are infeasible for the long tail of underrepresented languages. In this work, we investigate a more constrained question: in data-scarce settings, to what extent can linguistically similar pivot languages and few-shot demonstrations provide useful guidance for on-the-fly adaptation in LLMs? We study a data-efficient experimental setup that combines linguistically related pivot languages with few-shot in-context examples, without any parameter updates, and evaluate translation behavior under controlled conditions. Our analysis shows that while pivot-based prompting can yield improvements in certain configurations, particularly in settings where the target language is less well represented in the model's vocabulary, the gains are often modest and sensitive to few shot example construction. For closely related or better represented varieties, we observe diminishing or inconsistent gains. Our findings provide empirical guidance on how and when inference-time prompting and pivot-based examples can be used as a lightweight alternative to fine-tuning in low-resource translation settings.

</details>


### [159] [Arabic Morphosyntactic Tagging and Dependency Parsing with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16718)
*Mohamed Adel,Bashar Alhafni,Nizar Habash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 评估指令调优的大语言模型在标准阿拉伯语的形态句法标注和依存句法分析中的表现，发现提示设计和示例选择显著影响性能，专有模型接近监督基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型是否能生成明确的语言结构，特别是在具有丰富形态和正字法歧义的阿拉伯语中。

Method: 在零样本提示和基于检索的上下文学习设置下，使用阿拉伯语树库实例进行评估，并比较不同提示设计和示例选择的影响。

Result: 专有模型在特征级标注上接近监督基线，在依存句法分析上与专用解析器相当；基于检索的上下文学习改善了句法分析和分词效果。

Conclusion: 大语言模型能可靠捕捉部分阿拉伯语形态句法和句法结构，但在某些方面仍存在挑战，提示设计和示例选择对性能有重要影响。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform strongly on many NLP tasks, but their ability to produce explicit linguistic structure remains unclear. We evaluate instruction-tuned LLMs on two structured prediction tasks for Standard Arabic: morphosyntactic tagging and labeled dependency parsing. Arabic provides a challenging testbed due to its rich morphology and orthographic ambiguity, which create strong morphology-syntax interactions. We compare zero-shot prompting with retrieval-based in-context learning (ICL) using examples from Arabic treebanks. Results show that prompt design and demonstration selection strongly affect performance: proprietary models approach supervised baselines for feature-level tagging and become competitive with specialized dependency parsers. In raw-text settings, tokenization remains challenging, though retrieval-based ICL improves both parsing and tokenization. Our analysis highlights which aspects of Arabic morphosyntax and syntax LLMs capture reliably and which remain difficult.

</details>


### [160] [Probing Cultural Signals in Large Language Models through Author Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16749)
*Valentin Lafargue,Ariel Guerra-Adames,Emmanuelle Claeys,Elouan Vuichard,Jean-Michel Loubes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在零样本设置下评估大语言模型（LLM）从歌曲歌词中推断歌手性别和种族的能力，发现模型存在系统性文化偏见，倾向于默认北美族裔，且不同模型表现出显著的族裔偏差差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大语言模型在社会应用中可能携带的文化偏见，特别是对作者性别和种族的隐含假设。

Method: 在超过10,000首歌词上以零样本方式评估多个开源大语言模型的作者画像能力，并分析其预测分布与生成的推理过程；提出两种公平性指标（MAD和RD）量化族裔偏差。

Result: 发现大语言模型能非随机地推断作者性别与种族，表现出系统性文化倾向：多数模型偏向北美族裔，DeepSeek-1.5B更倾向亚洲族裔；Ministral-8B族裔偏见最强，Gemma-12B表现最均衡。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在作者画像任务中展现出显著的文化偏见，需通过新提出的公平性指标进行系统评估，以推动更公正的模型设计与部署。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in applications with societal impact, raising concerns about the cultural biases they encode. We probe these representations by evaluating whether LLMs can perform author profiling from song lyrics in a zero-shot setting, inferring singers' gender and ethnicity without task-specific fine-tuning. Across several open-source models evaluated on more than 10,000 lyrics, we find that LLMs achieve non-trivial profiling performance but demonstrate systematic cultural alignment: most models default toward North American ethnicity, while DeepSeek-1.5B aligns more strongly with Asian ethnicity. This finding emerges from both the models' prediction distributions and an analysis of their generated rationales. To quantify these disparities, we introduce two fairness metrics, Modality Accuracy Divergence (MAD) and Recall Divergence (RD), and show that Ministral-8B displays the strongest ethnicity bias among the evaluated models, whereas Gemma-12B shows the most balanced behavior. Our code is available on GitHub (https://github.com/ValentinLafargue/CulturalProbingLLM).

</details>


### [161] [TurnWise: The Gap between Single- and Multi-turn Language Model Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16759)
*Victoria Graf,Valentina Pyatkin,Nouha Dziri,Nathan Lambert,Hannaneh Hajishirzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为TurnWiseEval的新基准，用于评估语言模型的多轮对话能力，并引入了合成多轮数据的管道TurnWiseData。实验表明，使用多轮数据训练对提升多轮对话性能至关重要，仅加入1万条多轮对话数据即可在TurnWiseEval上实现12%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放训练和评估数据主要集中在单轮设置上，无法充分反映多轮对话的复杂性。为了填补这一多轮与单轮之间的差距，研究旨在开发能够准确衡量和增强语言模型多轮对话能力的方法。

Method: 提出了一种新的多轮对话能力评测基准TurnWiseEval，该基准可直接与单轮对话评估进行比较，并通过成对比较等效的单轮设置来隔离多轮特定的对话能力。同时，设计了一个名为TurnWiseData的合成多轮数据生成管道，以支持大规模生成多轮训练数据。

Result: 实验结果表明，在后训练阶段仅加入1万条多轮对话数据，就能使Olmo 3模型在TurnWiseEval基准上的表现提升12%，证明了多轮训练数据对提高多轮对话性能的关键作用。

Conclusion: 多轮对话训练数据对于提升语言模型在多轮交互中的表现至关重要，TurnWiseEval和TurnWiseData为未来多轮对话系统的研究提供了有效的评估手段和数据支持。

Abstract: Multi-turn conversations are a common and critical mode of language model interaction. However, current open training and evaluation data focus on single-turn settings, failing to capture the additional dimension of these longer interactions. To understand this multi-/single-turn gap, we first introduce a new benchmark, TurnWiseEval, for multi-turn capabilities that is directly comparable to single-turn chat evaluation. Our evaluation isolates multi-turn specific conversational ability through pairwise comparison to equivalent single-turn settings. We additionally introduce our synthetic multi-turn data pipeline TurnWiseData which allows the scalable generation of multi-turn training data. Our experiments with Olmo 3 show that training with multi-turn data is vital to achieving strong multi-turn chat performance, and that including as little as 10k multi-turn conversations during post-training can lead to a 12% improvement on TurnWiseEval.

</details>


### [162] [SpokenUS: A Spoken User Simulator for Task-Oriented Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16783)
*Jonggeun Lee,Junseong Pyo,Jeongmin Park,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了SpokenTOD数据集和基于其的SpokenUS用户模拟器，支持多种口语行为建模，提升了任务导向对话系统的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有口语任务导向对话数据集在规模和领域覆盖上有限，缺乏系统性的增强方法，难以充分模拟真实用户的多样性行为。

Method: 构建了包含52,390个对话和1,034小时语音的SpokenTOD数据集，涵盖跨轮槽位、打断、不流畅和情感语调四种口语行为；并设计了专门支持打断行为的SpokenUS用户模拟器架构。

Result: SpokenUS在目标覆盖率上媲美更大模型，在人类主观评分（MOS）上显著优于所有基线，能逐步披露槽值，更贴近人类行为。

Conclusion: SpokenUS及其数据集为训练和评估更鲁棒的口语对话系统提供了实用工具，验证了多样化口语行为对下游系统的挑战性。

Abstract: Robust task-oriented spoken dialogue agents require exposure to the full diversity of how people interact through speech. Building spoken user simulators that address this requires large-scale spoken task-oriented dialogue (TOD) data encompassing spoken user behaviors, yet existing datasets are limited in scale and domain coverage, with no systematic pipeline for augmenting them. To address this, we introduce \textbf{SpokenTOD}, a spoken TOD dataset of 52,390 dialogues and 1,034 hours of speech augmented with four spoken user behaviors -- cross-turn slots, barge-in, disfluency, and emotional prosody -- across diverse speakers and domains. Building on SpokenTOD, we present \textbf{SpokenUS}, a spoken user simulator grounded in TOD with a dedicated architecture for barge-in. SpokenUS achieves comparable goal coverage to significantly larger models while substantially outperforming all baselines in Human MOS, disclosing slot values gradually across the dialogue as humans do rather than front-loading them. Further analysis confirms that SpokenUS's spoken behaviors pose meaningful challenges to downstream agents, making it a practical tool for training and evaluating more robust spoken dialogue systems.

</details>


### [163] [Mediocrity is the key for LLM as a Judge Anchor Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16848)
*Shachar Don-Yehiya,Asaf Yehudai,Leshem Choshen,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统探讨了在“LLM-as-a-judge”评估范式中锚定模型选择对评估结果可靠性的影响，发现常用的最佳或最差性能模型作为锚点效果较差，并提出了改进的锚点选择指南和基准规模建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管“LLM-as-a-judge”广泛使用，但基于单一锚点的评估方法中锚点选择对结果可靠性的影响尚未充分研究。

Method: 通过在Arena-Hard-v2.0数据集上评估22种不同锚点，分析其与人类排名的相关性，量化锚点选择的影响效应大小，并进行功效分析以确定合适的基准规模。

Result: 发现极端表现的模型（最佳或最差）作为锚点会显著降低与人类排名的相关性；锚点选择的影响程度与裁判模型的选择相当；标准基准规模不足以可靠区分竞争模型。

Conclusion: 应避免使用极端性能模型作为锚点，推荐选择更具信息量的中间性能模型，并增加基准测试规模以提高评估的可靠性和有效性。

Abstract: The ``LLM-as-a-judge'' paradigm has become a standard method for evaluating open-ended generation. To address the quadratic scalability costs of pairwise comparisons, popular benchmarks like Arena-Hard and AlpacaEval compare all models against a single anchor. However, despite its widespread use, the impact of anchor selection on the reliability of the results remains largely unexplored. In this work, we systematically investigate the effect of anchor selection by evaluating 22 different anchors on the Arena-Hard-v2.0 dataset. We find that the choice of anchor is critical: a poor anchor can dramatically reduce correlation with human rankings. We identify that common anchor choices (best-performing and worst-performing models) make poor anchors. Because these extreme anchors are consistently better or worse than all other models, they are seldom indicative of the relative ranking of the models. We further quantify the effect size of anchor selection, showing it is comparable to the selection of a judge model. We conclude with actionable recommendations. First, we conduct a power analysis, and compute sufficient benchmark sizes for anchor-based evaluation, finding that standard benchmark sizes are insufficient for pairwise evaluation and fail to distinguish between competitive models reliably. Second, we provide guidelines for selecting informative anchors to ensure reliable and efficient evaluation practices.

</details>


### [164] [Online Experiential Learning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16856)
*Tianzhu Ye,Li Dong,Qingxiu Dong,Xun Wu,Shaohan Huang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出在线体验学习（OEL）框架，使大语言模型能从实际部署经验中持续改进，通过提取和整合交互轨迹中的可迁移知识，在无需访问用户环境的情况下实现模型的迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型主要依赖离线训练，无法利用实际部署过程中积累的丰富经验，限制了其持续改进能力。

Method: OEL框架分为两个阶段：首先从用户端收集的交互轨迹中提取可迁移的经验知识；然后通过策略内上下文蒸馏将这些知识整合到模型参数中，并循环迭代这两个阶段形成在线学习闭环。

Result: 在多个文本游戏环境中对不同规模的模型进行评估，OEL在多轮迭代中持续提升任务准确率和token效率，同时保持对分布外任务的性能；分析表明，提取的知识比原始轨迹更有效，且知识来源与策略模型之间的策略一致性对学习效果至关重要。

Conclusion: OEL为大语言模型提供了一种可行的在线持续学习路径，能够有效利用部署经验实现自我进化，具有较强的实用性和扩展潜力。

Abstract: The prevailing paradigm for improving large language models relies on offline training with human annotations or simulated environments, leaving the rich experience accumulated during real-world deployment entirely unexploited. We propose Online Experiential Learning (OEL), a framework that enables language models to continuously improve from their own deployment experience. OEL operates in two stages: first, transferable experiential knowledge is extracted and accumulated from interaction trajectories collected on the user side; second, this knowledge is consolidated into model parameters via on-policy context distillation, requiring no access to the user-side environment. The two stages are iterated to form an online learning loop, where the improved model collects higher-quality trajectories that yield richer experiential knowledge for subsequent rounds. We evaluate OEL on text-based game environments across multiple model scales and both thinking and non-thinking variants. OEL achieves consistent improvements over successive iterations, enhancing both task accuracy and token efficiency while preserving out-of-distribution performance. Our analysis further shows that extracted experiential knowledge is significantly more effective than raw trajectories, and that on-policy consistency between the knowledge source and the policy model is critical for effective learning.

</details>


### [165] [Chronos: Temporal-Aware Conversational Agents with Structured Event Retrieval for Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16862)
*Sahil Sen,Elias Lumer,Anmol Gulati,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Chronos是一种新型的时间感知记忆框架，通过将对话分解为带有时间范围和实体别名的事件元组，并结合动态提示与结构化日历索引，显著提升了大语言模型在长时间多轮对话中对时序敏感问题的检索与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统难以处理跨越数月交互中不断演变的时间相关事实与偏好，且缺乏有效的多跳、时间敏感型查询检索策略。

Method: 提出Chronos框架，将原始对话分解为带有解析后时间范围和实体别名的主谓宾事件元组，存储于结构化事件日历和保留完整上下文的对话轮次日历中；查询时通过动态提示生成定制化检索指令，指导模型进行时间过滤与多跳推理。

Result: 在LongMemEvalS基准测试中，使用8种大语言模型评估，Chronos Low准确率达92.60%，Chronos High达95.60%，较先前最优系统提升7.67%；消融实验显示事件日历贡献最大（+58.9%），其他组件提升15.5%-22.3%。

Conclusion: Chronos显著提升了大语言模型在长期对话中的时间感知记忆与复杂查询能力，成为当前最先进的解决方案，且即使低配版本也优于此前最强系统。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled conversational AI agents to engage in extended multi-turn interactions spanning weeks or months. However, existing memory systems struggle to reason over temporally grounded facts and preferences that evolve across months of interaction and lack effective retrieval strategies for multi-hop, time-sensitive queries over long dialogue histories. We introduce Chronos, a novel temporal-aware memory framework that decomposes raw dialogue into subject-verb-object event tuples with resolved datetime ranges and entity aliases, indexing them in a structured event calendar alongside a turn calendar that preserves full conversational context. At query time, Chronos applies dynamic prompting to generate tailored retrieval guidance for each question, directing the agent on what to retrieve, how to filter across time ranges, and how to approach multi-hop reasoning through an iterative tool-calling loop over both calendars. We evaluate Chronos with 8 LLMs, both open-source and closed-source, on the LongMemEvalS benchmark comprising 500 questions spanning six categories of dialogue history tasks. Chronos Low achieves 92.60% and Chronos High scores 95.60% accuracy, setting a new state of the art with an improvement of 7.67% over the best prior system. Ablation results reveal the events calendar accounts for a 58.9% gain on the baseline while all other components yield improvements between 15.5% and 22.3%. Notably, Chronos Low alone surpasses prior approaches evaluated under their strongest model configurations.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Simulation Distillation: Pretraining World Models in Simulation for Rapid Real-World Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15759)
*Jacob Levy,Tyler Westenbroek,Kevin Huang,Fernando Palafox,Patrick Yin,Shayegan Omidshafiei,Dong-Ki Kim,Abhishek Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Simulation Distillation (SimDist)的仿真到现实迁移框架，通过将仿真器中的结构先验蒸馏到潜在世界模型中，实现基于在线规划和监督式动力学微调的快速现实世界适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有仿真到现实迁移方法在真实机器人低数据场景下存在的探索困难和长视野信用分配问题。

Method: 通过将奖励模型和价值模型从仿真直接迁移到现实，提供来自原始感知的密集规划信号，并利用在线规划与监督式动力学微调进行现实适应，避免部署过程中的价值学习。

Result: 在精确操作和四足行走任务中，SimDist在数据效率、稳定性和最终性能上显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: SimDist通过将仿真中的结构先验有效转移至现实世界，将现实适应简化为短视野系统辨识，从而实现快速、稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: Simulation-to-real transfer remains a central challenge in robotics, as mismatches between simulated and real-world dynamics often lead to failures. While reinforcement learning offers a principled mechanism for adaptation, existing sim-to-real finetuning methods struggle with exploration and long-horizon credit assignment in the low-data regimes typical of real-world robotics. We introduce Simulation Distillation (SimDist), a sim-to-real framework that distills structural priors from a simulator into a latent world model and enables rapid real-world adaptation via online planning and supervised dynamics finetuning. By transferring reward and value models directly from simulation, SimDist provides dense planning signals from raw perception without requiring value learning during deployment. As a result, real-world adaptation reduces to short-horizon system identification, avoiding long-horizon credit assignment and enabling fast, stable improvement. Across precise manipulation and quadruped locomotion tasks, SimDist substantially outperforms prior methods in data efficiency, stability, and final performance. Project website and code: https://sim-dist.github.io/

</details>


### [167] [CorrectionPlanner: Self-Correction Planner with Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15771)
*Yihong Guo,Dongqiangzi Ye,Sijia Chen,Anqi Liu,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有自修正能力的自动驾驶规划器CorrectionPlanner，通过“提议-评估-修正”循环生成安全的运动令牌，显著降低了碰撞率并在多个基准上达到先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的规划器缺乏显式的自修正能力，一旦生成不安全动作便无法纠正，导致安全性不足。因此，需要一种能够在决策过程中动态修正不安全行为的规划方法。

Method: 提出CorrectionPlanner，将规划建模为运动令牌的自回归生成过程，引入一个学习得到的碰撞批评器来评估每个动作的安全性；若预测会碰撞，则保留不安全令牌作为自修正轨迹，并基于其继续生成直至输出安全动作。训练采用模仿学习结合基于模型的强化学习，利用预训练世界模型生成闭环仿真轨迹。

Result: 在Waymax上碰撞率降低超过20%，在nuPlan上取得当前最优的规划评分。

Conclusion: CorrectionPlanner通过引入类似语言模型推理链的自修正机制，在运动令牌空间中实现了有效的错误纠正，提升了自动驾驶规划的安全性和整体性能。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires safe planning, but most learning-based planners lack explicit self-correction ability: once an unsafe action is proposed, there is no mechanism to correct it. Thus, we propose CorrectionPlanner, an autoregressive planner with self-correction that models planning as motion-token generation within a propose, evaluate, and correct loop. At each planning step, the policy proposes an action, namely a motion token, and a learned collision critic predicts whether it will induce a collision within a short horizon. If the critic predicts a collision, we retain the sequence of historical unsafe motion tokens as a self-correction trace, generate the next motion token conditioned on it, and repeat this process until a safe motion token is proposed or the safety criterion is met. This self-correction trace, consisting of all unsafe motion tokens, represents the planner's correction process in motion-token space, analogous to a reasoning trace in language models. We train the planner with imitation learning followed by model-based reinforcement learning using rollouts from a pretrained world model that realistically models agents' reactive behaviors. Closed-loop evaluations show that CorrectionPlanner reduces collision rate by over 20% on Waymax and achieves state-of-the-art planning scores on nuPlan.

</details>


### [168] [Emergent Dexterity via Diverse Resets and Large-Scale Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15789)
*Patrick Yin,Tyler Westenbroek,Zhengyu Zhang,Joshua Tran,Ignacio Dagnino,Eeshani Shilamkar,Numfor Mbiziwo-Tiapo,Simran Bagaria,Xinlei Liu,Galen Mullins,Andrey Kolobov,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为\Method的新框架，通过系统性地重置仿真环境来提升强化学习在灵巧操作任务中的探索效率，实现了无需课程学习、人工示范或任务特定设计的通用且可扩展的机器人学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在复杂、长视野、接触丰富的操作任务中表现脆弱，依赖大量针对特定任务的设计，且难以随计算资源增加而持续提升性能。

Method: \Method利用仿真器重置机制，程序化生成多样化的机器人-物体交互初始状态，从而扩大状态空间覆盖范围，并通过增加计算资源直接提升行为多样性与策略性能。

Result: \Method在多种长视野灵巧操作任务上超越现有方法，展现出更广的初始条件适应范围，并能蒸馏出在真实世界零样本迁移中具有高成功率和鲁棒重试行为的视觉运动策略。

Conclusion: \Method为实现可扩展、稳健的模拟到真实机器人学习提供了一个简单有效的解决方案，显著提升了强化学习在复杂操作任务中的实用性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in massively parallel physics simulations has driven major progress in sim-to-real robot learning. However, current approaches remain brittle and task-specific, relying on extensive per-task engineering to design rewards, curricula, and demonstrations. Even with this engineering, they often fail on long-horizon, contact-rich manipulation tasks and do not meaningfully scale with compute, as performance quickly saturates when training revisits the same narrow regions of state space. We introduce \Method, a simple and scalable framework that enables on-policy reinforcement learning to robustly solve a broad class of dexterous manipulation tasks using a single reward function, fixed algorithm hyperparameters, no curricula, and no human demonstrations. Our key insight is that long-horizon exploration can be dramatically simplified by using simulator resets to systematically expose the RL algorithm to the diverse set of robot-object interactions which underlie dexterous manipulation. \Method\ programmatically generates such resets with minimal human input, converting additional compute directly into broader behavioral coverage and continued performance gains. We show that \Method\ gracefully scales to long-horizon dexterous manipulation tasks beyond the capabilities of existing approaches and is able to learn robust policies over significantly wider ranges of initial conditions than baselines. Finally, we distill \Method \ into visuomotor policies which display robust retrying behavior and substantially higher success rates than baselines when transferred to the real world zero-shot. Project webpage: https://omnireset.github.io

</details>


### [169] [Robust Dynamic Object Detection in Cluttered Indoor Scenes via Learned Spatiotemporal Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15826)
*Juan Rached,Yixuan Jia,Kota Kondo,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅使用LiDAR的框架，融合时间占用网格运动分割与学习到的鸟瞰图动态先验，显著提升了在复杂环境中对动态物体的检测召回率和F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于几何的LiDAR方法在动态物体靠近静态结构时易漏检，而视觉增强方法受限于检测类别封闭性和视场约束，难以应对新颖或视野外的障碍物。

Method: 设计了一种仅使用LiDAR的框架，融合基于时间的占用网格运动分割与学习到的鸟瞰图（BEV）动态先验；通过一个融合模块优先使用3D检测结果，并利用动态网格恢复因邻近静态物导致漏检的目标。

Result: 在高杂乱环境下的实验表明，相比当前最先进方法，该方法将召回率提高了28.67%，F1分数提高18.50%，同时保持了相当的精度和位置误差。

Conclusion: 所提出的LiDAR-only框架通过融合时序运动信息与学习先验，有效提升了复杂场景中动态物体检测的鲁棒性和完整性。

Abstract: Reliable dynamic object detection in cluttered environments remains a critical challenge for autonomous navigation. Purely geometric LiDAR pipelines that rely on clustering and heuristic filtering can miss dynamic obstacles when they move in close proximity to static structure or are only partially observed. Vision-augmented approaches can provide additional semantic cues, but are often limited by closed-set detectors and camera field-of-view constraints, reducing robustness to novel obstacles and out-of-frustum events. In this work, we present a LiDAR-only framework that fuses temporal occupancy-grid-based motion segmentation with a learned bird's-eye-view (BEV) dynamic prior. A fusion module prioritizes 3D detections when available, while using the learned dynamic grid to recover detections that would otherwise be lost due to proximity-induced false negatives. Experiments with motion-capture ground truth show our method achieves 28.67% higher recall and 18.50% higher F1 score than the state-of-the-art in substantially cluttered environments while maintaining comparable precision and position error.

</details>


### [170] [Gaze-Aware Task Progression Detection Framework for Human-Robot Interaction Using RGB Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15951)
*Linlin Cheng,Koen Hindriks,Artem V. Belopolsky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需校准的、基于RGB相机的注视估计框架，用于检测人机交互中的任务进展，利用机器人内置摄像头监测用户注意力模式，通过三个兴趣区域（平板、机器人面部和其他）识别任务完成，实验结果显示该方法在舒适性、社交存在感和自然性方面显著优于按钮交互，证明了仅用RGB相机实现直观低成本的人机交互的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数注视检测方法依赖专用硬件，限制了其在日常场景中的应用；而基于外观的注视估计虽可降低硬件需求，但在人机交互中的实用性尚不明确，因此需要探索一种低成本、易部署的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种无需校准的框架，使用机器人内置单目RGB相机结合最先进的注视估计技术，定义三个兴趣区域（AOI）：平板、机器人面部和其他区域，通过系统化参数优化平衡检测准确率与交互延迟，利用用户在任务界面与机器人面部之间的视线转移来判断任务完成。

Result: 在“第一天上班”场景中验证框架有效性，任务完成检测准确率达77.6%；相比按钮交互，响应延迟略高，但信息保留能力相当，并在舒适性、社交存在感和感知自然性方面显著提升；多数参与者未意识到主动使用眼动进行交互，表明注视作为交流线索具有高度直觉性。

Conclusion: 基于RGB相机的注视估计可用于构建直观、低成本、自然且吸引人的人机交互系统，无需额外硬件即可实现有效的任务状态检测，具有实际部署潜力。

Abstract: In human-robot interaction (HRI), detecting a human's gaze helps robots interpret user attention and intent. However, most gaze detection approaches rely on specialized eye-tracking hardware, limiting deployment in everyday settings. Appearance-based gaze estimation methods remove this dependency by using standard RGB cameras, but their practicality in HRI remains underexplored. We present a calibration-free framework for detecting task progression when information is conveyed via integrated display interfaces. The framework uses only the robot's built-in monocular RGB camera (640x480 resolution) and state-of-the-art gaze estimation to monitor attention patterns. It leverages natural behavior, where users shift focus from task interfaces to the robot's face to signal task completion, formalized through three Areas of Interest (AOI): tablet, robot face, and elsewhere. Systematic parameter optimization identifies configurations that balance detection accuracy and interaction latency. We validate our framework in a "First Day at Work" scenario, comparing it to button-based interaction. Results show a task completion detection accuracy of 77.6%. Compared to button-based interaction, the proposed system exhibits slightly higher response latency but preserves information retention and significantly improves comfort, social presence, and perceived naturalness. Notably, most participants reported that they did not consciously use eye movements to guide the interaction, underscoring the intuitive role of gaze as a communicative cue. This work demonstrates the feasibility of intuitive, low-cost, RGB-only gaze-based HRI for natural and engaging interactions.

</details>


### [171] [ExpertGen: Scalable Sim-to-Real Expert Policy Learning from Imperfect Behavior Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15956)
*Zifan Xu,Ran Gong,Maria Vittoria Minniti,Ahmet Salih Gundogdu,Eric Rosen,Kausik Sivakumar,Riedana Yan,Zixing Wang,Di Deng,Peter Stone,Xiaohan Zhang,Karl Schmeckpeper*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExpertGen是一个自动化在仿真中学习专家策略的框架，通过结合扩散模型与强化学习，利用不完美的演示生成高质量、可迁移的机器人控制策略，并在真实环境中实现高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取大规模高质量的真实世界机器人数据成本高昂，而现有方法难以兼顾数据规模与行为鲁棒性，因此需要一种可在仿真中自动生成专家策略并支持高效sim-to-real迁移的方法。

Method: 首先使用扩散策略模型在不完美演示（如LLM生成或人类提供）上训练行为先验；然后冻结该策略，通过强化学习优化其初始噪声以提升任务成功率，从而在保持行为安全性的同时实现稀疏奖励下的有效学习；最后将学到的状态策略通过DAgger蒸馏为视觉运动策略用于实际部署。

Result: 在工业装配任务中达到90.5%的整体成功率，在长视野操作任务中达到85%，优于所有基线方法；所学策略表现出灵巧控制能力，并对不同初始状态和失败情况具有鲁棒性；成功实现了从仿真到真实机器人的迁移。

Conclusion: ExpertGen能够高效生成通用且鲁棒的行为克隆策略，无需奖励工程即可实现高性能sim-to-real迁移，为大规模机器人策略学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Learning generalizable and robust behavior cloning policies requires large volumes of high-quality robotics data. While human demonstrations (e.g., through teleoperation) serve as the standard source for expert behaviors, acquiring such data at scale in the real world is prohibitively expensive. This paper introduces ExpertGen, a framework that automates expert policy learning in simulation to enable scalable sim-to-real transfer. ExpertGen first initializes a behavior prior using a diffusion policy trained on imperfect demonstrations, which may be synthesized by large language models or provided by humans. Reinforcement learning is then used to steer this prior toward high task success by optimizing the diffusion model's initial noise while keep original policy frozen. By keeping the pretrained diffusion policy frozen, ExpertGen regularizes exploration to remain within safe, human-like behavior manifolds, while also enabling effective learning with only sparse rewards. Empirical evaluations on challenging manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that ExpertGen reliably produces high-quality expert policies with no reward engineering. On industrial assembly tasks, ExpertGen achieves a 90.5% overall success rate, while on long-horizon manipulation tasks it attains 85% overall success, outperforming all baseline methods. The resulting policies exhibit dexterous control and remain robust across diverse initial configurations and failure states. To validate sim-to-real transfer, the learned state-based expert policies are further distilled into visuomotor policies via DAgger and successfully deployed on real robotic hardware.

</details>


### [172] [Safety Case Patterns for VLA-based driving systems: Insights from SimLingo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16013)
*Gerhard Yu,Fuyuki Ishikawa,Oluwafemi Odu,Alvine Boaye Belle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RAISE的安全案例设计方法，用于支持基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）的自动驾驶系统的安全性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA系统虽然具有灵活性和响应指令的能力，但引入自然语言输入可能导致不可预测的危险行为，因此需要确保其安全性以建立信任。

Method: 提出了RAISE方法，包括针对指令驱动系统的新型安全模式、扩展的危害分析与风险评估（HARA）以及安全案例构建技术。

Result: 通过SimLingo案例研究展示了该方法可用于构建严谨、基于证据的安全声明。

Conclusion: RAISE为新兴的VLA型自动驾驶系统提供了一种有效的安全案例设计途径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA)-based driving systems represent a significant paradigm shift in autonomous driving since, by combining traffic scene understanding, linguistic interpretation, and action generation, these systems enable more flexible, adaptive, and instruction-responsive driving behaviors. However, despite their growing adoption and potential to support socially responsible autonomous driving while understanding high-level human instructions, VLA-based driving systems may exhibit new types of hazardous behaviors. Such as the addition of natural language inputs (e.g., user or navigation instructions) into the multimodal control loop, which may lead to unpredictable and unsafe behaviors that could endanger vehicle occupants and pedestrians. Hence, assuring the safety of these systems is crucial to help build trust in their operations. To support this, we propose a novel safety case design approach called RAISE. Our approach introduces novel patterns tailored to instruction-based driving systems such as VLA-based driving systems, an extension of Hazard Analysis and Risk Assessment (HARA) detailing safe scenarios and their outcomes, and a design technique to create the safety cases of VLA-based driving systems. A case study on SimLingo illustrates how our approach can be used to construct rigorous, evidence-based safety claims for this emerging class of autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [173] [Geometry-Aligned LLM Fine-Tuning for Sequential Narrow-Opening Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16028)
*Al Jaber Mahmud,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于几何对齐的LLM微调框架，用于生成刚体在连续狭窄通道中的运动规划路径，通过双层训练 pipeline 实现高成功率和长视野几何推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决刚体在多个连续狭窄通道中运动规划所需的长视野几何推理问题，传统方法难以处理因前期位形限制导致的后续可达性约束。

Method: 采用双层训练 pipeline：首先基于人类示范和结构化失败反馈进行失败驱动的LoRA监督微调；然后使用几何验证的Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）进一步优化LoRA适配器，结合模型基规划器稠密化路径并以几何奖励评估连续运动可行性。

Result: 在模拟环境中取得定量和定性验证结果，本方法在分布内和分布外场景下均达到最高成功率，并能生成有利于后续通过的出口姿态，展现出长视野几何推理能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效提升了LLM在复杂几何约束下的运动规划能力，实现了机器可读、几何可行且跨开口协调的路径生成。

Abstract: We study rigid-body motion planning through multiple sequential narrow openings, which requires long-horizon geometric reasoning because the configuration used to traverse an early opening constrains the set of reachable configurations for subsequent ones. To achieve this, we propose a geometry-aligned large language model (LLM) fine-tuning framework that generates fixed-length, machine-readable waypoint sequences that are both geometrically feasible and coordinated across openings. Our approach uses a bi-level training pipeline. First, we perform failure-driven LoRA supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on human demonstrations, which incorporates structured failure feedback to teach the model common failure modes and enforce the output format. Second, we refine the same LoRA adapters using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with geometric verification: each sampled waypoint sequence is densified by a model-based planner and scored with a deterministic geometry-derived reward to achieve continuous-motion feasibility. To validate the effectiveness of our proposed method, we provide both quantitative and qualitative results from simulations. Our method achieves the highest success rate in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution environments and qualitatively exhibits long-horizon geometric reasoning by selecting exit poses that facilitate entry into subsequent openings.

</details>


### [174] [Compact Optical Single-axis Joint Torque Sensor Using Redundant Photo-Reflectors and Quadratic-Programming Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16040)
*Hyun-Bin Kim,Byeong-Il Ham,Kyung-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于非接触式光电反射器的关节扭矩传感器，用于精确的关节级扭矩控制和安全的物理交互，具有高精度、低噪声和良好的温度稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于电流传感器的扭矩估计在协作机器人中由于齿轮箱粘滞/摩擦和电流-扭矩非线性，在低扭矩尤其是静态条件下精度较差，因此需要一种更精确的非接触式扭矩传感方法。

Method: 采用光电方式测量弹性结构的微小变形，并使用四方向排列的冗余光电反射器阵列提高灵敏度和信噪比；提出基于二次规划的校准方法，利用冗余信息抑制噪声、提升分辨率；并通过有理函数拟合补偿温度引起的零漂。

Result: 实验显示z轴扭矩测量最大误差为0.083%FS，RMS误差为0.0266 Nm；在1kHz下无滤波时3 sigma分辨率达0.0224 Nm，较最小二乘法提升2.14倍；温度补偿有效抑制了MCU自热和电机发热导致的零漂。

Conclusion: 该传感器实现了高精度、高分辨率和良好的温度稳定性，显著提升了低扭矩下的跟踪性能和扰动鲁棒性，适用于对安全性与控制精度要求高的协作机器人系统。

Abstract: This study proposes a non-contact photo-reflector-based joint torque sensor for precise joint-level torque control and safe physical interaction. Current-sensor-based torque estimation in many collaborative robots suffers from poor low-torque accuracy due to gearbox stiction/friction and current-torque nonlinearity, especially near static conditions. The proposed sensor optically measures micro-deformation of an elastic structure and employs a redundant array of photo-reflectors arranged in four directions to improve sensitivity and signal-to-noise ratio. We further present a quadratic-programming-based calibration method that exploits redundancy to suppress noise and enhance resolution compared to least-squares calibration. The sensor is implemented in a compact form factor (96 mm diameter, 12 mm thickness). Experiments demonstrate a maximum error of 0.083%FS and an RMS error of 0.0266 Nm for z-axis torque measurement. Calibration tests show that the proposed calibration achieves a 3 sigma resolution of 0.0224 Nm at 1 kHz without filtering, corresponding to a 2.14 times improvement over the least-squares baseline. Temperature chamber characterization and rational fitting based compensation mitigate zero drift induced by MCU self heating and motor heat. Motor-level validation via torque control and admittance control confirms improved low torque tracking and disturbance robustness relative to current-sensor-based control.

</details>


### [175] [The Era of End-to-End Autonomy: Transitioning from Rule-Based Driving to Large Driving Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16050)
*Eduardo Nebot,Julie Stephany Berrio Perez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了自动驾驶从模块化规则驱动系统向端到端学习系统的转变，分析了大型驾驶模型（LDMs）的架构、部署策略及安全影响，并讨论其在自动驾驶及其他具身智能系统中的商业主导趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶技术的发展，传统模块化方法难以应对复杂真实场景中的长尾问题，促使行业转向端到端学习系统以提升整体性能和适应性。

Method: 通过回顾经典感知-规划-控制架构的演进，分析特斯拉FSD、Rivian统一智能平台、NVIDIA Cosmos等最新系统的设计与部署实践，总结端到端学习在实际应用中的优势与挑战。

Result: 端到端学习系统在处理现实世界驾驶长尾场景方面表现出更强能力，监督式端到端驾驶（如L2++）正成为主流商用方向，驾驶员角色逐渐转变为安全监督者。

Conclusion: 端到端学习正在成为自动驾驶的主导范式，并有望推广至人形机器人等其他具身AI系统，推动整个智能体领域的发展。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is undergoing a shift from modular rule based pipelines toward end to end (E2E) learning systems. This paper examines this transition by tracing the evolution from classical sense perceive plan control architectures to large driving models (LDMs) capable of mapping raw sensor input directly to driving actions. We analyze recent developments including Tesla's Full Self Driving (FSD) V12 V14, Rivian's Unified Intelligence platform, NVIDIA Cosmos, and emerging commercial robotaxi deployments, focusing on architectural design, deployment strategies, safety considerations and industry implications. A key emerging product category is supervised E2E driving, often referred to as FSD (Supervised) or L2 plus plus, which several manufacturers plan to deploy from 2026 onwards. These systems can perform most of the Dynamic Driving Task (DDT) in complex environments while requiring human supervision, shifting the driver's role to safety oversight. Early operational evidence suggests E2E learning handles the long tail distribution of real world driving scenarios and is becoming a dominant commercial strategy. We also discuss how similar architectural advances may extend beyond autonomous vehicles (AV) to other embodied AI systems, including humanoid robotics.

</details>


### [176] [Ultrafast Sampling-based Kinodynamic Planning via Differential Flatness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16059)
*Thai Duong,Clayton W. Ramsey,Zachary Kingston,Wil Thomason,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AkinoPDF的快速并行采样式运动规划方法，适用于广泛的一类微分平坦机器人系统，通过将规划问题转换到平坦输出空间来实现快速、精确的动力学可行轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高自由度机器人（如机械臂）的运动规划中，传统基于采样的方法难以高效处理动力学约束，导致规划时间过长。现有快速并行方法仅限于几何路径，无法满足动态可行性需求。

Method: 利用微分平坦性将原状态空间中的运动规划问题映射到平坦输出空间，在该空间中解析求解两点边值问题并进行动力学积分，再将轨迹转换回原空间得到闭式动力学可行轨迹，并采用SIMD并行化实现快速验证。

Result: 所提方法可在微秒至毫秒级内完成规划，支持包括机械臂、地面和空中机器人在内的多种系统，在复杂和动态环境中经过仿真和实物实验验证具有高效性和精确性。

Conclusion: AkinoPDF为微分平坦机器人系统提供了一种快速、精确且兼容各类采样式规划器的运动规划方案，显著提升了动力学约束下的规划效率。

Abstract: Motion planning under dynamics constraints, i.e., kinodynamic planning, enables safe robot operation by generating dynamically feasible trajectories that the robot can accurately track. For high-\dof robots such as manipulators, sampling-based motion planners are commonly used, especially for complex tasks in cluttered environments. However, enforcing constraints on robot dynamics in such planners requires solving either challenging two-point boundary value problems (BVPs) or propagating robot dynamics over time, both of which are computational bottlenecks that drastically increase planning times. Meanwhile, recent efforts have shown that sampling-based motion planners can generate plans in microseconds using parallelization, but are limited to geometric paths. This paper develops AkinoPDF, a fast parallelized sampling-based kinodynamic motion planning technique for a broad class of differentially flat robot systems, including manipulators, ground and aerial vehicles, and more. Differential flatness allows us to transform the motion planning problem from the original state space to a flat output space, where an analytical time-parameterized solution of the BVP and dynamics integration can be obtained. A trajectory in the flat output space is then converted back to a closed-form dynamically feasible trajectory in the original state space, enabling fast validation via ``single instruction, multiple data" parallelism. Our method is fast, exact, and compatible with any sampling-based motion planner. We extensively verify the effectiveness of our approach in both simulated benchmarks and real experiments with cluttered and dynamic environments, requiring mere microseconds to milliseconds of planning time.

</details>


### [177] [Large Reward Models: Generalizable Online Robot Reward Generation with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16065)
*Yanru Wu,Weiduo Yuan,Ang Qi,Vitor Guizilini,Jiageng Mao,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用基础视觉语言模型（VLM）作为在线奖励生成器来实现机器人操作策略在线优化的框架，无需人工设计奖励函数，在零样本情况下显著提升了初始模仿学习策略的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在机器人操作中受限于难以设计通用且可扩展的奖励函数，本文旨在利用VLM的强大泛化能力自动生成多维度、可靠的奖励信号，以克服该瓶颈。

Method: 基于最先进的VLM构建一个鲁棒且可扩展的奖励模型，并在大规模多源数据集上进行训练；利用VLM从当前视觉观测中生成包含过程、完成度和时间对比的多维度奖励信号；结合模仿学习初始化的基线策略，通过闭环强化学习进行在线策略优化。

Result: 在具有挑战性的长视野操作任务上验证了方法的有效性，仅用30次强化学习迭代即显著提升初始策略的成功率，且奖励模型在测试环境中完全以零样本方式运行。

Conclusion: VLM生成的奖励信号能够有效指导机器人策略的在线优化，避免了繁琐的手工奖励工程，实现了高效、无需人工干预的策略改进，为机器人学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has shown great potential in refining robotic manipulation policies, yet its efficacy remains strongly bottlenecked by the difficulty of designing generalizable reward functions. In this paper, we propose a framework for online policy refinement by adapting foundation VLMs into online reward generators. We develop a robust, scalable reward model based on a state-of-the-art VLM, trained on a large-scale, multi-source dataset encompassing real-world robot trajectories, human-object interactions, and diverse simulated environments. Unlike prior approaches that evaluate entire trajectories post-hoc, our method leverages the VLM to formulate a multifaceted reward signal comprising process, completion, and temporal contrastive rewards based on current visual observations. Initializing with a base policy trained via Imitation Learning (IL), we employ these VLM rewards to guide the model to correct sub-optimal behaviors in a closed-loop manner. We evaluate our framework on challenging long-horizon manipulation benchmarks requiring sequential execution and precise control. Crucially, our reward model operates in a purely zero-shot manner within these test environments. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly improves the success rate of the initial IL policy within just 30 RL iterations, demonstrating remarkable sample efficiency. This empirical evidence highlights that VLM-generated signals can provide reliable feedback to resolve execution errors, effectively eliminating the need for manual reward engineering and facilitating efficient online refinement for robot learning.

</details>


### [178] [Towards the Vision-Sound-Language-Action Paradigm: The HEAR Framework for Sound-Centric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16086)
*Chang Nie,Tianchen Deng,Guangming Wang,Zhe Liu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Vision-Sound-Language-Action (VSLA) 框架，以实现基于连续听觉感知的机器人操作，引入HEAR系统并通过音频世界模型和流式上下文建模提升对环境声音的实时理解与响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型大多将声音作为静态提示或仅关注语音，忽视了动态环境中关键声音在任务执行中的状态验证作用，导致盲执行区间内重要声学事件丢失。

Method: 提出VSLA范式，构建HEAR框架，包含流式Historizer维持因果音频上下文、Envisioner进行多感官推理、Advancer作为音频世界模型预测未来音频码、Realizer生成平滑动作块，并构建OpenX-Sound数据集和HEAR-Bench评估基准。

Result: 实验表明，HEAR在声音为中心的操作任务中显著优于现有方法，验证了因果持续性和显式时序学习对鲁棒操作的重要性。

Conclusion: VSLA范式和HEAR框架为具身智能体的多感官融合提供了有效路径，推动机器人在动态环境中实现更精细的感知与交互。

Abstract: While recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have begun to incorporate audio, they typically treat sound as static pre-execution prompts or focus exclusively on human speech. This leaves a significant gap in real-time, sound-centric manipulation where fleeting environmental acoustics provide critical state verification during task execution. Consequently, key sounds are easily missed due to low-frequency updates or system latency. This problem is exacerbated by action chunking with open-loop execution, which creates a Blind Execution Interval where acoustic events are lost between discrete audio observation windows. Recognizing the necessity of continuous auditory awareness, we formalize Vision-Sound-Language-Action (VSLA) as a continuous control paradigm conditioned on vision, streaming audio, language, and proprioception under delayed decision loops. As an instantiation, we introduce HEAR, a VSLA framework integrating four components: (i) a streaming Historizer to maintain a compact, causal audio context across execution gaps; (ii) an Envisioner adapted from omni foundation models to reason over multi-sensory inputs; (iii) an Advancer, formulated as an audio world model, to learn temporal dynamics by predicting near-future audio codes; and (iv) a flow-matching Realizer policy to generate smooth action chunks. To address the scarcity of pretraining data and evaluations for VSLA, we construct OpenX-Sound for pretraining, alongside HEAR-Bench, the first sound-centric manipulation benchmark with strict causal timing rules. Our results suggest that robust sound-centric manipulation necessitates causal persistence and explicit temporal learning. This framework provides a practical step toward multi-sensory foundation models for embodied agents, enabling robots to perceive and interact with dynamic environments. Code and videos are available at https://hear.irmv.top.

</details>


### [179] [SE(3)-LIO: Smooth IMU Propagation With Jointly Distributed Poses on SE(3) Manifold for Accurate and Robust LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16118)
*Gunhee Shin,Seungjae Lee,Jei Kong,Youngwoo Seo,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SE(3)流形的IMU传播与不确定性感知运动补偿方法，用于提升激光-惯性里程计（LIO）的精度，并通过多组实验验证了SE(3)-LIO的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IMU传播方法在运动预测中未能有效融合旋转变化对平移的影响，且在运动补偿中忽略预测位姿间的相关性导致相对变换不确定性增大。

Method: 将6自由度位姿统一在SE(3)流形上进行表示与传播，并考虑预测位姿间的相关性来精确建模相对变换的不确定性，进而提出SE(3)-LIO框架，结合不确定性感知运动补偿（UAMC）优化激光雷达数据的畸变校正。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在轨迹估计精度上优于现有方法，尤其在高动态和复杂运动场景下表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过在SE(3)流形上统一建模位姿传播并引入相关性感知的不确定性估计，显著提升了激光-惯性里程计的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: In estimating odometry accurately, an inertial measurement unit (IMU) is widely used owing to its high-rate measurements, which can be utilized to obtain motion information through IMU propagation. In this paper, we address the limitations of existing IMU propagation methods in terms of motion prediction and motion compensation. In motion prediction, the existing methods typically represent a 6-DoF pose by separating rotation and translation and propagate them on their respective manifold, so that the rotational variation is not effectively incorporated into translation propagation. During motion compensation, the relative transformation between predicted poses is used to compensate motion-induced distortion in other measurements, while inherent errors in the predicted poses introduce uncertainty in the relative transformation. To tackle these challenges, we represent and propagate the pose on SE(3) manifold, where propagated translation properly accounts for rotational variation. Furthermore, we precisely characterize the relative transformation uncertainty by considering the correlation between predicted poses, and incorporate this uncertainty into the measurement noise during motion compensation. To this end, we propose a LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO), referred to as SE(3)-LIO, that integrates the proposed IMU propagation and uncertainty-aware motion compensation (UAMC). We validate the effectiveness of SE(3)-LIO on diverse datasets. Our source code and additional material are available at: https://se3-lio.github.io/.

</details>


### [180] [SignNav: Leveraging Signage for Semantic Visual Navigation in Large-Scale Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16166)
*Jian Sun,Yuming Huang,He Li,Shuqi Xiao,Shenyan Guo,Maani Ghaffari,Qingbiao Li,Chengzhong Xu,Hui Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的具身导航任务SignNav，要求智能体通过解读标识中的语义线索进行决策，并构建了LSI-Dataset用于训练和评估。为应对动态变化的语义提示和稀疏标识分布带来的挑战，提出了一种时空感知Transformer（START）模型，结合空间与时间感知模块，在无预建地图的情况下实现了80%的成功率和0.74 NDTW的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在大型室内环境中常利用标识提供的语义线索进行导航，但这一能力在具身导航领域尚未被充分探索。现有方法通常依赖预建地图或不考虑动态语义线索，限制了实际应用。因此，需要研究能理解并推理标识语义信息的自主导航方法。

Method: 提出SignNav任务，构建包含动态语义提示和稀疏标识设置的LSI-Dataset；设计Spatial-Temporal Aware Transformer（START）模型，其中空间感知模块将标识语义定位到物理空间，时间感知模块捕捉历史状态与当前观测之间的长程依赖关系；采用基于DAgger的两阶段训练策略进行端到端训练。

Result: 在val-unseen数据集上达到80%的成功率（SR）和0.74 NDTW，显著优于基线方法；实验证明了模型在动态语义变化和稀疏标识条件下的鲁棒性；真实场景部署验证了其无需预建地图的实际可用性。

Conclusion: SignNav为具身导航引入了更贴近人类行为的语义线索理解任务，START模型有效解决了动态与稀疏环境下的导航挑战，推动了无地图依赖的智能体在复杂室内环境中的实用化进程。

Abstract: Humans routinely leverage semantic hints provided by signage to navigate to destinations within novel Large-Scale Indoor (LSI) environments, such as hospitals and airport terminals. However, this capability remains underexplored within the field of embodied navigation. This paper introduces a novel embodied navigation task, SignNav, which requires the agent to interpret semantic hint from signage and reason about the subsequent action based on current observation. To facilitate research in this domain, we construct the LSI-Dataset for the training and evaluation of various SignNav agents. Dynamically changing semantic hints and sparse placement of signage in LSI environments present significant challenges to the SignNav task. To address these challenges, we propose the Spatial-Temporal Aware Transformer (START) model for end-to-end decision-making. The spatial-aware module grounds the semantic hint of signage into physical world, while the temporal-aware module captures long-range dependencies between historical states and current observation. Leveraging a two-stage training strategy with Dataset Aggregation (DAgger), our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, recording an 80% Success Rate (SR) and 0.74 NDTW on val-unseen split. Real-world deployment further demonstrates the practicality of our method in physical environment without pre-built map.

</details>


### [181] [Enforcing Task-Specified Compliance Bounds for Humanoids via Anisotropic Lipschitz-Constrained Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16180)
*Zewen He,Yoshihiko Nakamura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种各向异性Lipschitz约束策略（ALCP），用于增强强化学习中人形机器人运动控制的合规性和稳定性，通过将任务空间刚度上限映射到策略雅可比矩阵的状态相关约束，实现了方向相关的合规控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在实现人形机器人合规控制时缺乏与实际物理系统中可量化合规性要求的明确联系，且传统基于模型的方法难以直接应用。

Method: 提出各向异性Lipschitz约束策略（ALCP），将任务空间的刚度上界转化为对策略雅可比矩阵的状态依赖性Lipschitz式约束，并在强化学习训练中通过铰链平方谱范数惩罚来实施该约束。

Result: 实验表明，ALCP能有效提升人形机器人行走的稳定性与抗冲击能力，减少振荡和能量消耗。

Conclusion: ALCP为强化学习中的合规控制提供了具有物理可解释性的方向相关约束方法，显著改善了复杂运动控制的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has demonstrated substantial potential for humanoid bipedal locomotion and the control of complex motions. To cope with oscillations and impacts induced by environmental interactions, compliant control is widely regarded as an effective remedy. However, the model-free nature of RL makes it difficult to impose task-specified and quantitatively verifiable compliance objectives, and classical model-based stiffness designs are not directly applicable. Lipschitz-Constrained Policies (LCP), which regularize the local sensitivity of a policy via gradient penalties, have recently been used to smooth humanoid motions. Nevertheless, existing LCP-based methods typically employ a single scalar Lipschitz budget and lack an explicit connection to physically meaningful compliance specifications in real-world systems. In this study, we propose an anisotropic Lipschitz-constrained policy (ALCP) that maps a task-space stiffness upper bound to a state-dependent Lipschitz-style constraint on the policy Jacobian. The resulting constraint is enforced during RL training via a hinge-squared spectral-norm penalty, preserving physical interpretability while enabling direction-dependent compliance. Experiments on humanoid robots show that ALCP improves locomotion stability and impact robustness, while reducing oscillations and energy usage.

</details>


### [182] [PanguMotion: Continuous Driving Motion Forecasting with Pangu Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16196)
*Quanhao Ren,Yicheng Li,Nan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于连续驾驶场景的运动预测框架PanguMotion，利用大语言模型中的Transformer模块增强特征表示，并在经过RealMotion策略处理的Argoverse 2数据集上进行实验，以更好地模拟真实驾驶环境中的时间连续性和历史上下文关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常独立处理离散驾驶场景，忽略了实际驾驶环境中固有的时间连续性和历史上下文相关性，因此需要一种能够建模连续场景的运动预测方法。

Method: 将Pangu-1B大语言模型中的Transformer模块集成到自动驾驶运动预测架构中，作为特征增强组件，构建PanguMotion框架，并采用RealMotion数据重组策略将Argoverse 2数据集中的每个独立场景转换为连续序列。

Result: 在经RealMotion处理的Argoverse 2数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性，能够更好地捕捉长时间依赖和历史上下文信息。

Conclusion: PanguMotion通过引入大语言模型的结构优势，有效提升了连续驾驶场景下的运动预测性能，增强了对真实驾驶环境的建模能力。

Abstract: Motion forecasting is a core task in autonomous driving systems, aiming to accurately predict the future trajectories of surrounding agents to ensure driving safety. Existing methods typically process discrete driving scenes independently, neglecting the temporal continuity and historical context correlations inherent in real-world driving environments. This paper proposes PanguMotion, a motion forecasting framework for continuous driving scenarios that integrates Transformer blocks from the Pangu-1B large language model as feature enhancement modules into autonomous driving motion prediction architectures. We conduct experiments on the Argoverse 2 datasets processed by the RealMotion data reorganization strategy, transforming each independent scene into a continuous sequence to mimic real-world driving scenarios.

</details>


### [183] [Enabling Dynamic Tracking in Vision-Language-Action Models via Time-Discrete and Time-Continuous Velocity Feedforward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16218)
*Johannes Hechtl,Philipp Schmitt,Georg von Wichert,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中引入速度前馈项以解决工业机器人中合规性与响应性之间的权衡问题，提出了两种提取速度目标的方法：基于有限差分的离散方法和基于立方B样条的连续轨迹方法，并验证了其在高接触复杂任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行为克隆方法仅预测低频离散位姿，缺乏速度和加速度前馈信息，导致依赖高刚度控制以实现精确跟踪，牺牲了接触安全性与柔顺性。

Method: 提出两种从VLA模型中提取速度目标的方法：一是基于时间离散的有限差分近似，二是采用连续的立方B样条动作空间，生成C2连续轨迹；两种方法均不依赖特定模型，仅需调整遥操作、数据处理和底层控制器。

Result: 在‘立方体入槽’这一高接触复杂任务上对π₀.₅模型进行微调并评估，结果显示有限差分法显著提升执行速度，B样条方法保持高成功率并支持更平滑的高阶导数输出。

Conclusion: 引入速度前馈可有效缓解柔顺性与响应性之间的矛盾，所提两种方法具有模型无关性，易于集成到现有VLA架构中，为高频率柔顺控制提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: While vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown great promise for robot manipulation, their deployment on rigid industrial robots remains challenging due to the inherent trade-off between compliance and responsiveness. Standard Behavior Cloning (BC) approaches predict discrete poses at low frequencies, omitting the velocity and acceleration feedforward terms typically used by low-level compliant controllers. This requires to rely on high stiffness for accurate tracking, thereby sacrificing safe contact dynamics. In this paper, we demonstrate the importance of integrating velocity feedforward terms into VLA policies to resolve this trade-off. We propose two methods for extracting velocity targets from VLAs: a time-discrete finite-difference approximation that serves as a highly effective bridge for existing models, and a continuous Cubic B-Spline action space that natively yields $C^2$ continuous trajectories for high-frequency control. Crucially, both approaches are strictly model-agnostic and compatible with any standard action-chunking architecture, requiring modifications only to teleoperation, data processing, and the low-level controller. We fine-tune the $π_{0.5}$ model and evaluate both of our approaches on a demanding, contact-rich cube-in-hole task. Our results indicate that incorporating the velocity feedforward term via finite differences significantly improves task execution speed, while the continuous B-Spline approach maintains high overall success rates and provides a foundation for smoother higher-order derivatives without compromising compliance.

</details>


### [184] [PA-LVIO: Real-Time LiDAR-Visual-Inertial Odometry and Mapping with Pose-Only Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16228)
*Hailiang Tang,Tisheng Zhang,Liqiang Wang,Xin Ding,Man Yuan,Xiaoji Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PA-LVIO的实时LiDAR-视觉-惯性里程计方法，采用仅姿态的束调整框架，实现高精度、高效率的状态估计与建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足智能交通系统中对实时、高精度导航与建图的迫切需求，克服现有LVIO方法在效率和漂移方面的局限。

Method: 提出一种仅姿态的束调整（PA）框架，融合LiDAR、视觉和IMU数据；引入无需边缘化的帧到地图（F2M）测量模型，并采用IMU为中心的在线时空标定实现像素级LiDAR-相机对齐。

Result: 在多个公共和私有数据集上进行了28个序列、超过50公里轨迹的实验，结果表明PA-LVIO在里程计精度和建图质量方面优于或媲美现有最先进方法，并可在桌面PC和机载ARM计算机上实现实时运行。

Conclusion: PA-LVIO是一种高效、准确且适用于多平台的实时LiDAR-视觉-惯性里程计方案，能够生成高质量的彩色点云地图，具备良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Real-time LiDAR-visual-inertial odometry and mapping is crucial for navigation and planning tasks in intelligent transportation systems. This study presents a pose-only bundle adjustment (PA) LiDAR-visual-inertial odometry (LVIO), named PA-LVIO, to meet the urgent need for real-time navigation and mapping. The proposed PA framework for LiDAR and visual measurements is highly accurate and efficient, and it can derive reliable frame-to-frame constraints within multiple frames. A marginalization-free and frame-to-map (F2M) LiDAR measurement model is integrated into the state estimator to eliminate odometry drifts. Meanwhile, an IMU-centric online spatial-temporal calibration is employed to obtain a pixel-wise LiDAR-camera alignment. With accurate estimated odometry and extrinsics, a high-quality and RGB-rendered point-cloud map can be built. Comprehensive experiments are conducted on both public and private datasets collected by wheeled robot, unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV), and handheld devices with 28 sequences and more than 50 km trajectories. Sufficient results demonstrate that the proposed PA-LVIO yields superior or comparable performance to state-of-the-art LVIO methods, in terms of the odometry accuracy and mapping quality. Besides, PA-LVIO can run in real-time on both the desktop PC and the onboard ARM computer.

</details>


### [185] [Industrial cuVSLAM Benchmark & Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16240)
*Charbel Abi Hana,Kameel Amareen,Mohamad Mostafa,Dmitry Slepichev,Hesam Rabeti,Zheng Wang,Mihir Acharya,Anthony Rizk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对物流环境中移动机器人导航的视觉里程计（VO）和视觉SLAM（VSLAM）系统进行了全面基准评估，结果表明基于cuVSLAM前端与自定义后端结合的混合架构具有最佳性能，并在NVIDIA Jetson平台上成功部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升移动机器人在真实物流环境中的导航精度，需要对现有VO/VSLAM系统进行系统性评估，以确定最优方案并指导实际部署。

Method: 通过在受控轨迹（平移、旋转及复合运动）和大规模生产设施（约1.7公里）数据集上，使用Vicon动捕系统和LiDAR-SLAM作为真值参考，采用绝对位姿误差（APE）对多种视觉里程计方法进行评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，cuVSLAM前端与自定义SLAM后端组成的混合架构在建图精度上表现最优；该方案在NVIDIA Jetson平台上的部署也验证了其实际可行性。

Conclusion: cuVSLAM作为核心VO组件具有优越性能，推荐集成到机器人系统中以提升导航精度。

Abstract: This work presents a comprehensive benchmark evaluation of visual odometry (VO) and visual SLAM (VSLAM) systems for mobile robot navigation in real-world logistical environments. We compare multiple visual odometry approaches across controlled trajectories covering translational, rotational, and mixed motion patterns, as well as a large-scale production facility dataset spanning approximately 1.7 km. Performance is evaluated using Absolute Pose Error (APE) against ground truth from a Vicon motion capture system and a LiDAR-based SLAM reference. Our results show that a hybrid stack combining the cuVSLAM front-end with a custom SLAM back-end achieves the strongest mapping accuracy, motivating a deeper integration of cuVSLAM as the core VO component in our robotics stack. We further validate this integration by deploying and testing the cuVSLAM-based VO stack on an NVIDIA Jetson platform.

</details>


### [186] [MG-Grasp: Metric-Scale Geometric 6-DoF Grasping Framework with Sparse RGB Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16270)
*Kangxu Wang,Siang Chen,Chenxing Jiang,Shaojie Shen,Yixiang Dai,Guijin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需深度传感器的6自由度抓取框架MG-Grasp，利用双视图3D基础模型从稀疏RGB图像中重建度量尺度和多视角一致的稠密点云，实现高质量的物体抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单视角RGB-D抓取检测依赖深度传感器，而纯RGB方法因几何表示不准确难以满足物理可靠的机器人操作需求。

Method: 利用带有相机内参/外参的双视图3D基础模型，从稀疏RGB图像重建度量尺度和多视角一致的稠密点云，并生成稳定的6-DoF抓取姿态。

Result: 在GraspNet-1Billion数据集和真实场景实验中，MG-Grasp在基于RGB的6-DoF抓取方法中达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: MG-Grasp是一种高效的无深度6-DoF抓取框架，在无需深度传感器的情况下仍能实现高精度和物理可靠的抓取。

Abstract: Single-view RGB-D grasp detection remains a com- mon choice in 6-DoF robotic grasping systems, which typically requires a depth sensor. While RGB-only 6-DoF grasp methods has been studied recently, their inaccurate geometric repre- sentation is not directly suitable for physically reliable robotic manipulation, thereby hindering reliable grasp generation. To address these limitations, we propose MG-Grasp, a novel depth- free 6-DoF grasping framework that achieves high-quality object grasping. Leveraging two-view 3D foundation model with camera intrinsic/extrinsic, our method reconstructs metric- scale and multi-view consistent dense point clouds from sparse RGB images and generates stable 6-DoF grasp. Experiments on GraspNet-1Billion dataset and real world demonstrate that MG-Grasp achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) grasp performance among RGB-based 6-DoF grasping methods.

</details>


### [187] [GenZ-LIO: Generalizable LiDAR-Inertial Odometry Beyond Indoor--Outdoor Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16273)
*Daehan Lee,Hyungtae Lim,Seongjun Kim,Soonbin Rho,Changhyeon Lee,Sanghyun Park,Junwoo Hong,Eunseon Choi,Hyunyoung Jo,Soohee Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GenZ-LIO的通用激光雷达-惯性里程计框架，能够适应室内外不同空间尺度场景，通过自适应体素下采样、混合度量状态更新和体素剪枝匹配策略，实现了鲁棒且高效的位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR-inertial odometry方法在面对室内到室外等不同空间尺度场景切换时，因点云密度变化大而导致鲁棒性和计算效率下降，缺乏跨尺度泛化能力。

Method: 1）受PID控制器启发，采用反馈控制自适应调节体素大小，使体素化后的点数趋于与尺度相关的设定值；2）提出混合度量状态更新，联合使用点到面和点到点残差以缓解几何约束不足导致的退化问题；3）引入体素剪枝的对应搜索策略，剔除无希望的体素候选者以减少计算开销。

Result: 实验表明GenZ-LIO在狭窄室内、开阔室外及过渡环境中均能实现鲁棒的里程计估计，并提升计算效率。

Conclusion: GenZ-LIO通过自适应下采样、混合残差建模和高效匹配策略，有效提升了LiDAR-inertial系统在多尺度环境下的鲁棒性与实时性，具备良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Light detection and ranging (LiDAR)-inertial odometry (LIO) enables accurate localization and mapping for autonomous navigation in various scenes. However, its performance remains sensitive to variations in spatial scale, which refers to the spatial extent of the scene reflected in the distribution of point ranges in a LiDAR scan. Transitions between confined indoor and expansive outdoor spaces induce substantial variations in point density, which may reduce robustness and computational efficiency. To address this issue, we propose GenZ-LIO, a LIO framework generalizable across both indoor and outdoor environments. GenZ-LIO comprises three key components. First, inspired by the principle of the proportional-integral-derivative (PID) controller, it adaptively regulates the voxel size for downsampling via feedback control, driving the voxelized point count toward a scale-informed setpoint while enabling stable and efficient processing across varying scene scales. Second, we formulate a hybrid-metric state update that jointly leverages point-to-plane and point-to-point residuals to mitigate LiDAR degeneracy arising from directionally insufficient geometric constraints. Third, to alleviate the computational burden introduced by point-to-point matching, we introduce a voxel-pruned correspondence search strategy that discards non-promising voxel candidates and reduces unnecessary computations. Experimental results demonstrate that GenZ-LIO achieves robust odometry estimation and improved computational efficiency across confined indoor, open outdoor, and transitional environments. Our code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [188] [Agile Interception of a Flying Target using Competitive Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16279)
*Timothée Gavin,Simon Lacroix,Murat Bronz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于竞争性强化学习的敏捷无人机拦截方法，使用PPO算法在高保真仿真环境中训练拦截者与目标无人机的策略，并通过低层控制实现快速飞行，最终在真实室内场景中验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了有效拦截高速机动的无人机，传统方法难以应对复杂动态环境，因此需要更智能、自适应的策略。

Method: 将拦截问题建模为竞争性强化学习问题，采用PPO算法分别训练拦截方和目标方的策略；使用集成真实四旋翼动力学和基于JAX的低层控制架构的高保真仿真环境进行并行化训练。

Result: 所提方法在捕获率、捕获时间和碰撞率方面优于常见启发式基线方法，并在真实室内飞行实验中成功验证了策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于竞争性强化学习的方法能够有效实现敏捷无人机之间的拦截任务，具备向现实世界迁移的潜力。

Abstract: This article presents a solution to intercept an agile drone by another agile drone carrying a catching net. We formulate the interception as a Competitive Reinforcement Learning problem, where the interceptor and the target drone are controlled by separate policies trained with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). We introduce a high-fidelity simulation environment that integrates a realistic quadrotor dynamics model and a low-level control architecture implemented in JAX, which allows for fast parallelized execution on GPUs. We train the agents using low-level control, collective thrust and body rates, to achieve agile flights both for the interceptor and the target. We compare the performance of the trained policies in terms of catch rate, time to catch, and crash rate, against common heuristic baselines and show that our solution outperforms these baselines for interception of agile targets. Finally, we demonstrate the performance of the trained policies in a scaled real-world scenario using agile drones inside an indoor flight arena.

</details>


### [189] [OGScene3D: Incremental Open-Vocabulary 3D Gaussian Scene Graph Mapping for Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16301)
*Siting Zhu,Ziyun Lu,Guangming Wang,Chenguang Huang,Yongbo Chen,I-Ming Chen,Wolfram Burgard,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OGScene3D的开放词汇场景理解系统，能够增量式实现准确的3D语义建图与场景图构建，适用于机器人在逐步探索环境中进行持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预先构建完整的3D语义地图来生成场景图，难以应用于环境逐步探索的机器人场景，限制了其实际适用性。

Method: 提出基于置信度的高斯语义表示方法，结合层次化3D语义优化策略和长期全局优化机制，并通过2D-3D语义一致性与高斯渲染贡献进行联合优化；同时设计渐进式图构建方法，动态更新节点与语义关系。

Result: 在多个常用数据集和真实场景中的实验表明，该方法在开放词汇场景理解任务中实现了更优的语义建图精度和场景图一致性。

Conclusion: OGScene3D能够在不依赖完整先验地图的情况下，实现鲁棒、增量式的3D语义建图与场景图构建，显著提升机器人在复杂环境中的场景理解能力。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary scene understanding is crucial for robotic applications, enabling robots to comprehend complex 3D environmental contexts and supporting various downstream tasks such as navigation and manipulation. However, existing methods require pre-built complete 3D semantic maps to construct scene graphs for scene understanding, which limits their applicability in robotic scenarios where environments are explored incrementally. To address this challenge, we propose OGScene3D, an open-vocabulary scene understanding system that achieves accurate 3D semantic mapping and scene graph construction incrementally. Our system employs a confidence-based Gaussian semantic representation that jointly models semantic predictions and their reliability, enabling robust scene modeling. Building on this representation, we introduce a hierarchical 3D semantic optimization strategy that achieves semantic consistency through local correspondence establishment and global refinement, thereby constructing globally consistent semantic maps. Moreover, we design a long-term global optimization method that leverages temporal memory of historical observations to enhance semantic predictions. By integrating 2D-3D semantic consistency with Gaussian rendering contribution, this method continuously refines the semantic understanding of the entire scene.Furthermore, we develop a progressive graph construction approach that dynamically creates and updates both nodes and semantic relationships, allowing continuous updating of the 3D scene graphs. Extensive experiments on widely used datasets and real-world scenes demonstrate the effectiveness of our OGScene3D on open-vocabulary scene understanding.

</details>


### [190] [ADAPT: Adaptive Dual-projection Architecture for Perceptive Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16328)
*Shuo Shao,Tianchen Huang,Wei Gao,Shiwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ADAPT是一种用于敏捷人形机器人在复杂3D环境中运动的自适应感知架构，通过双投影表示环境并学习感知范围，实现高效、鲁棒的零样本迁移控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖固定的感知配置，难以在感知精度与计算效率之间取得平衡，限制了人形机器人在复杂环境中的敏捷运动能力。

Method: 提出ADAPT架构，使用水平高程图表示地形几何，垂直距离图表示可通行空间，并将感知空间范围作为可学习的动作，动态调整感知视野以适应不同场景。

Result: 相比基于体素的基线方法，ADAPT显著降低了观测维度和计算开销，加速了训练过程，并成功实现了在Unitree G1人形机器人上的零样本迁移，表现出更强的环境适应能力。

Conclusion: ADAPT通过自适应双投影感知架构，有效提升了人形机器人在多样化3D环境中的运动鲁棒性与计算效率，为复杂场景下的自主行走提供了新思路。

Abstract: Agile humanoid locomotion in complex 3D en- vironments requires balancing perceptual fidelity with com- putational efficiency, yet existing methods typically rely on rigid sensing configurations. We propose ADAPT (Adaptive dual-projection architecture for perceptive traversal), which represents the environment using a horizontal elevation map for terrain geometry and a vertical distance map for traversable- space constraints.
